### 2026-08-15 (session 2) — Phase 3 implementation + live verification: Auth Pages, plus a client-requested recurring-bug fix
- **What was implemented:**
  1. **Phase 3 (as scoped):** dedicated `/login` and `/signup` routes (`App.jsx`), a permanent `/auth` legacy redirect preserving query params and router state, route-driven mode in `Auth.jsx` (with back/forward and direct-nav support), signup fields split into First Name/Last Name + relabeled "Work Email" + new optional Company field, submit CTA changed to "Create Free Account", `firstName`/`lastName`/`company` persisted to Firestore for both email/password and Google signup (`backend/routes/users.js`'s `PUT /profile` extended to accept the new fields), and every hardcoded `/auth` link across the frontend updated to point directly at `/login` or `/signup`.
  2. **Client-requested alongside Phase 3 (screenshot of a live "Account data unavailable" error on `/dashboard`):** root-caused and fixed the actual recurring bug behind this — previously (2026-08-12) only a *downstream* Firestore-lookup failure was made retryable; `verifyIdToken()` itself failing transiently (the same failure class documented 2026-08-02) was never covered and fell through to a dead-end 401.   3. **Found during live verification, fixed:** a genuine pre-existing race condition in `GET /profile`'s auto-create (non-merge `.set()`, clobberable by a concurrent create) — rewritten with `db.runTransaction()`.

### 2026-08-15 (session 1) — Phase 2 implementation + live verification: Brand Token & Design System Reconciliation
- **What was implemented:** Resolved the brand-palette conflict (client confirmed `#FD4403`/`#002A64` as authoritative, with evidence — see `PHASES.md` Phase 2). Added semantic Tailwind tokens (`muted`, `success`, `warning`, `error`, `info`) formalizing already-dominant conventions. Found and fixed the real underlying bug the conflict-resolution work surfaced: **417 occurrences of literal Tailwind default `orange-*` classes across 28 files** (rendering a generic muted orange instead of the true brand orange) — replaced sitewide with `brand-*`. Fixed 2 raw hex color drifts (`Home.jsx`'s hero mockup, `EnterpriseOnboarding.jsx`'s stale white-label defaults). Normalized a `yellow-*`/`amber-*` warning-color inconsistency (25 occurrences, 6 files). Added `focus-visible` ring states to all 3 shared button classes (previously only `.input` had one).
- **Phase/tasks completed:** `PHASES.md` Phase 2 — Brand Token & Design System Reconciliation. Marked Completed with full implementation + verification detail in that file.
- **Files modified:** `frontend/tailwind.config.js`, `frontend/src/index.css`, and 28 `.jsx` files (see `git diff --stat` — primarily mechanical class-name substitutions, 2 files with additional targeted hex fixes).
- **Important implementation decisions:** Did NOT apply the external spec's typography/radius/danger-button numbers, since the client's confirmation was about the CURRENT system being authoritative, not the spec — only filled genuine gaps and fixed genuine drift. Did NOT mass-rename existing green/red/blue/gray usages (hundreds of occurrences) since they were already internally consistent and non-brand-conflicting — new semantic tokens were added as aliases for future use instead. Did NOT run a repo-wide `prettier --write` on the touched files, since `prettier --check` showed ~53 of ~55 frontend files were already not prettier-clean before this session (a pre-existing, deliberately-deferred repo-wide condition per `CLAUDE.md`) — forcing it would have buried the functional diff in unrelated whitespace noise.
- **Tests/checks performed and results:** `npm run lint` (frontend) 0 errors/15 warnings (pre-existing, unrelated). `npm run build` succeeded. `npm test` (Vitest) 4/4 passing. **Live browser verification** (Playwright, real Chromium, both dev servers running, throwaway QA account created in and deleted from the real Firebase project): Home/Pricing/Auth/Dashboard/Settings(+Security+Billing) at desktop+mobile — zero console errors, zero failed HTTP requests in the final clean run; screenshots confirmed correct brand-orange rendering everywhere, intact mobile layouts, and a working focus-visible ring (verified via computed `box-shadow: rgb(255,255,255) 0 0 0 2px, rgb(253,68,3) 0 0 0 4px`).
- **Bugs/blockers encountered:** An intermediate live-test run hit the backend's own auth rate-limiter (429s) from repeated test-script iterations — not a regression, resolved by restarting the backend to clear in-memory rate-limit state; the final clean run confirms zero errors. A focus-ring check initially sampled mid-CSS-transition (looked like a near-invisible ring) — fixed by waiting for the 200ms transition to settle before sampling.
- **Remaining work / minor findings (not blockers, not fixed):** `Pricing.jsx`'s `COLOR_MAP` has confusingly-named keys (`blue`→brand classes, `purple`→amber classes) — visually harmless, flagged for a future cleanup, out of this phase's scope.

### 2026-08-14 (session 3) — Phase 1 live verification against the real Anthropic API (no code changes)
- **What was done:** Controlled live smoke test per explicit user request: 15 real image files (`docs/baseline/*.png`) run through the real `analyzeImages()` and `generateReport()` via a temporary, throwaway script (created and fully deleted by end of session — confirmed via `git status`, no residue). No code was modified. 3 live runs total, plus one deliberate isolation run (zero photos) to root-cause an unexpected finding.
- **Results — Phase 1 mechanics (all passed, all 3 runs):** 15/15 photos analyzed every time; `imagesFailed: 0`, `imagesSkipped: 0`; exactly 2 real API calls per run (10+5 images, matching `VISION_BATCH_SIZE`); both batches ran concurrently (~12.3-12.8s total wall-clock, confirming the concurrency limiter, not sequential ~25s); zero timeout/API/parse errors across all runs.
- **Separate finding (root-caused, NOT a Phase 1 regression):** generated reports consistently omit Section 9. Isolation test (zero photos, `imageAnalysis: null`) reproduced the identical result, proving the cause lies entirely in `generateReport()`/`buildReportPrompt()` — files Phase 1 never touched. Most likely cause: existing fixed `maxTokens: 4096` budget exhausted before the last section; the codebase already has a dedicated repair (`ensureLossSummary()`) for an analogous risk on Section 7 but nothing for Section 9. Not fixed in this session — reporting only, per explicit instruction not to change code.
- **Also inspected (per explicit request):** `POST /analyze-images`'s separate 20-image cap — confirmed it does not conflict with the main 100-photo `/generate` flow (different endpoint, unused by the frontend UI, undocumented), though it is a latent inconsistency for any direct API caller of that specific endpoint.
- **Files touched:** none (product code). Documentation only: this file, `PHASES.md` (Phase 1 detail updated with live-verification results), `PROGRESS.md` (new changelog entry `[2026-08-14c]`).

### 2026-08-14 (session 2) — Phase 1 implementation: Full Photo-Set AI Analysis Coverage
- **What was implemented:** Fixed the critical bug where `backend/services/aiService.js:233`'s `images.slice(0, 10)` silently discarded all but the first 10 of up to 100 uploaded photos before AI analysis. `analyzeImages()` was rewritten to split all valid images into 10-image batches, process them with a concurrency cap of 3 via a small wave-based worker pool (no new dependency), and merge every batch's result through a new pure `aggregateBatchResults()` function — damages concatenated, review-items deduped, severity escalated to the worst seen across batches, square footage summed, and `totalImagesAnalyzed` always reflecting the true count. A failed or timed-out batch is caught individually and reported via new `imagesFailed`/`imagesSkipped` fields instead of being silently dropped or falsely counted as analyzed.
- **Phase/tasks completed:** `PHASES.md` Phase 1 — Critical Fix: Full Photo-Set AI Analysis Coverage. Marked Completed in both the Phase Summary Table and the Phase 1 detail section (implementation summary added there).
- **Files created:** `backend/test/image-analysis-batching.test.js` (15 tests).
- **Files modified:** `backend/services/aiService.js` (batching/aggregation rewrite of `analyzeImages`, new exports `aggregateBatchResults`/`VISION_BATCH_SIZE`), `backend/config/anthropic.js` (added a `timeout` option to `analyzeImages`, passed through to the Anthropic SDK's per-request options so a hung batch aborts after 90s instead of hanging indefinitely).
- **Important implementation decisions:**
  - Batch size kept at 10 (the previously-proven-safe per-call size) rather than attempting a larger single batch — reduces risk of hitting Anthropic payload/size limits with large photo sets.
  - Concurrency capped at 3 concurrent batches — a deliberate middle ground between latency (wall-clock time for 100 photos) and Anthropic rate-limit risk; not empirically tuned against the real API in this session (mocks only).
  - Testability achieved via a `callVisionApi` dependency-injection parameter on `analyzeImages()` rather than a module-mocking framework, matching this codebase's existing pure-function test style (see `crm-analytics.test.js`, `webhook-service.test.js`) and avoiding Node's experimental `--experimental-test-module-mocks` flag entirely.
  - Deliberately did NOT touch: the separate `POST /analyze-images` endpoint's own 20-image multer cap (a different, supplementary endpoint, out of this bug's scope); any async/queue infrastructure (intentionally deferred to Phase 7 — this fix stays synchronous within the existing request/response cycle).
- **Tests/checks performed and results:** `npm test` (backend): 55/55 passing (0 failures, 0 regressions in the 40 pre-existing tests). `npm run lint`: 0 errors, 38 warnings (identical to the pre-existing baseline — no new warnings). New/touched files run through `prettier --write` per `CLAUDE.md` convention for touched files.
- **Bugs/blockers encountered:** None — implementation went as planned, no open question from `PHASES.md`'s Phase 1 section needed resolving before this could proceed.
- **Remaining work for this phase:** A manual/live smoke test generating one real report with ~50-100 real photos against the actual Anthropic API is recommended but was not performed (would require real API credentials/cost and is a "live check," not a unit test) — flagged as a follow-up, not a blocker to calling Phase 1 code-complete.
- **Updated phase statuses:** Phase 1 → Completed in `PHASES.md` and `CONTEXT.md`. All other phases unchanged (Not Started).
- **Exact next recommended step:** Await explicit user instruction on which phase to implement next. Per `PHASES.md`'s dependency ordering, Phase 2 (Brand Token Reconciliation) has an open question blocking it (which palette is authoritative); Phases 3 and 4 (Auth Pages, Dashboard Home View) have no blocking open questions and are reasonable next candidates if the user wants to continue down the roadmap in order.
